  - rationale: Adjusting for critical defensive phase. Reduced Growth from 85 to 75 to shift city production toward military units and nuclear infrastructure. Increased Naval from 65 to 75 and NavalRecon from 55 to 65 in response to Spanish Dreadnought and Destroyer attacks on our Ironclads near Assur (Zone -1069 shows enemy naval strength of 2171). Spanish naval pressure requires stronger counter-fleet to protect our coastal cities. Raised Production to maximum 100. Maintaining Defense/Antiair at 95 for paratrooper defense against Spanish airborne units, Science/Spaceship at 100 for victory timeline, Nuke at 100 for Manhattan Project completion in 6 turns, and UseNuke at 95. We must survive 18 turns until Nuclear Fission completes and can deploy nuclear deterrent.

- original:
  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Nebuchadnezzar II, leader of Babylon (Player 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Babylon
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Bowman, Replacing Archer (Requires technology: Calendar)
        - Unique Unit: Sabum Kibitum, Replacing Spearman (Requires technology: Bronze Working)
        - Unique Building: Walls of Babylon, Replacing Walls (Requires technology: Construction)
        - Unique Building: Etemenanki, Replacing National Monument (Requires technology: Drama and Poetry)
        - Civilization Ability: Receive a Free Great Scientist when you discover Writing, and Great Scientists are earned 50% faster than normal. Investing Gold in Buildings reduces their Production cost by an additional 15%.
      - Leader: Nebuchadnezzar II
      - PlayerID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Refrigeration: Allows Work Boats to construct an Offshore Platform, and increases Trade Route maximum travel distance.
        Completing it would unlock: Electronics, Computers
      - Advanced Ballistics: Allows you to build the Nuclear Missile, a frightening weapon that requires Uranium, and is capable of destroying units and cities. Also allows you to build the Guided Missile, an inexpensive single-use Unit that can damage enemy Units, and the SS Booster, one of the Spaceship parts necessary to win a Science Victory.
        Completing it would unlock: Lasers, Stealth
      - Mobile Tactics: Allows you to build the Mechanized Infantry, a fast and versatile late game Unit that doesn't require any resources.
        Completing it would unlock: Stealth
      
      ## Policies
      - Workers' Faculties (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Worker Faculties: Factories increase City Science output by 10%. +100% Production towards Factories, and receive 5 Free Factories in your Empire.
      - People's Army (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): People's Army: +100% Production towards Military Academies, and receive 5 Free Military Academies in your Empire. Public Schools produce +5 Culture and +2 Happiness.
      - Equality (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness in every City and +1 Happiness for every 15 Citizens in a City.
        - * -1 Unhappiness from and Distress, Poverty, Illiteracy, and Boredom in all Cities.
      - Shadow Networks (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * Receive 25% of Science generated by Allied City-States.
        - * +3% Culture in Capital for every 100 Spy Points ever accumulated (up to 30%).
        - * +3 Science from Constabularies and Police Stations.
        - * +1 Science from Specialists.
      - Splendor (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * Expending a Great Person grants 50 Culture, scaling with Era.
        - * State Treasury built in Capital (+4 Gold, +2 Culture to all Monuments, Gardens, and Baths; 1 Merchant Slot).
      - Communism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Communism: Gold cost of investing in buildings reduced by 25%. +20% Production when building Wonders.
      - Sovereignty (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * Culture cost of tiles reduced by 20% (exponentially) in all Cities.
        - * Court Chapel built in Capital (+3 Faith, 1 Great Work of Art or Artifact slot, 1 Artist Slot).
      - Double Agents (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Double Agents: Receive 200 Spy Points. Gain 125 Science when you identify a foreign Spy, scaling with Era.
      - Patriotic War (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Patriotic War: Land Units gain +20% Strength when in or adjacent to a City. When you conquer a City for the first time, all Combat Units within 3 Tiles of the City are fully healed. Can build T-34s.
      - Refinement (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +2 Culture from Great Works of Art.
        - * 1 Specialist in all cities does not produce Unhappiness from Urbanization.
        - * +1 Culture from Specialists.
      - Humanism (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +3 Faith from Great Works of Writing.
        - * -25% Golden Age Points needed to trigger a Golden Age.
        - * +1 Happiness from all Guilds.
      - Industry (New Branch):
        - Industry provides bonuses to empires focused on Gold and Production.
        - Adopting Industry grants:
        - * +2 Trade Routes.
        - * -5% Gold needed for purchases.
        - Each Industry policy unlocked grants:
        - * -5% Gold needed for purchases.
        - Adopting all Policies in Industry grants:
        - * Unlocks building Broadway.
        - * +3 Happiness per unique owned Luxury Resource.
        - * +2 Production and Gold from Specialists.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Merchants with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Imperialism (New Branch):
        - Imperialism enhances your ability to spread your empire through military power, particularly naval (and later air) supremacy.
        - Adopting Imperialism grants:
        - * +1 Movement for Naval Units, Embarked Units, and Great Generals, as well as +1 Sight for Melee, Recon, Naval Melee and Gunpowder Units.
        - * +10% Production toward Military Units, -10% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Each Imperialism policy unlocked grants:
        - * +5% Production for Military Units, -5% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Adopting all policies in Imperialism grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Pentagon.
        - * All Naval and Air units gain the Banzai! Promotion (become stronger as they take damage).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Admirals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Fealty (New Branch):
        - Fealty enhances your ability to defend and leverage your Majority Religion.
        - Adopting Fealty grants:
        - * Can purchase Monasteries with Faith (+3 Food, +3 Science, +2 Faith).
        - * -25% Faith costs for purchasing Buildings, Missionaries, and Inquisitors.
        - Each Fealty policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Faith and Strength in every City.
        - Adopting all Policies in Fealty grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Red Fort.
        - * Your Tourism modifier for Shared Religion is 50% stronger.
        - * Cities that follow your Majority Religion generate +3 Production, Gold, Science, and Culture.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Artists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Authority (New Branch):
        - Authority will greatly benefit warlike and expansionist civilizations.
        - Adopting Authority grants:
        - * +25% combat bonus VS Barbarians and receive announcements when Barbarian Camps spawn in revealed territory.
        - * Receive 25 Culture when you clear Barbarian Camps, scaling with Era.
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 50% of its Strength.
        - * +1 Production in every City.
        - Each Authority policy unlocked grants:
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 10% of its Strength.
        - Adopting all policies in Authority grants:
        - * Unlocks building Alhambra.
        - * Allows the purchasing of Free Companies, Foreign Legions, and Mercenaries as their prerequisite technologies are researched.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Generals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.

      - content: # Strategies
        Strategies: existing strategic decisions from you.
        
        ## Persona
        - VictoryCompetitiveness: 5
        - WonderCompetitiveness: 9
        - MinorCivCompetitiveness: 10
        - Boldness: 1

        - DenounceWillingness: 5
        - Forgiveness: 7
        - Meanness: 4
        - Neediness: 4
        - Chattiness: 3
        - DeceptiveBias: 4
        - Rationale: Turn 365: Maintaining minimum Boldness (1) and WarBias (1) despite being at war. This signals to other civilizations (especially Russia, Austria, Morocco) that our war posture is purely defensive, not expansionist. We were forced into war through defensive pact with Netherlands - we did not seek it. High WarmongerHate (10) and Forgiveness (7) positions us for rapid peace settlement when possible. Need to avoid appearing threatening to prevent dogpile scenario where multiple civs attack us while we're weak.
        
        ## Technology
        - Next: None
        
        ## Policy
        - Next: Double Agents (Policy)
        - Rationale: Turn 372: Double Agents remains optimal. Agent Kishar's death leaves us with only 5 active agents. This tenet provides 200 Spy Points for rapid agent recruitment and 125 Science per identified spy. Combined with Great Leap Forward (+100% espionage science), each identified spy yields 250 Science - a massive boost while we research Nuclear Fission and Rocketry. This compensates for our 1113 beakers/turn deficit against Spain's technological lead.
        
        ## Relationships
        
        ### Greece
        - Public: 20
        - Private: 15
        - Rationale: Turn 291: Maintain strong friendship with Greece. They provide 40 GPT from our Jade trade deal, have adopted our religion-friendly stance, and share DoF. Despite religious differences, they are a reliable economic partner and buffer state. Keep positive relations to ensure continued trade income and potential future cooperation.
        - UpdatedTurn: 291
        
        ### Spain
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 0
        - Rationale: Turn 372: Maintaining 0/0 relationship with Spain. Austria's peace deal with Spain (events 1-21) means Spain can now redirect military pressure. Morocco at 87% war weariness will likely peace out soon, further freeing Spanish forces. However, Spain still shows 0% war score against us, indicating they haven't committed to full invasion yet. Preserving diplomatic flexibility for peace negotiations if Spain becomes overstretched, while preparing defenses for potential assault.
        - UpdatedTurn: 372
        
        ### Russia
        - Public: 100
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: Turn 372: Maintaining maximum 100/100 relationship with Russia. With Austria making peace with Spain, Russia becomes even more critical as our primary military counterweight. Russia has 5092 military strength vs Spain's 17687, and is fighting Spain at -2% war score. Russia's continued resistance prevents Spain from focusing entirely on the Order alliance (us, Netherlands, Morocco). Combined strength of Russia (5092) + us (3397) + Netherlands (2648) = 11137, still less than Spain but credible defensive coalition.
        - UpdatedTurn: 373
        
        ### Austria
        - Public: -25
        - Private: -30
        - Rationale: Turn 372: Austria's peace deal with Spain is concerning. While we understand they needed peace (fighting Spain while being Freedom ideology surrounded by Order powers), this frees Spanish forces. Slightly increasing caution from -20/-25 to -25/-30. Austria has 17 delegates vs our 12, and their Proposal to repeal our Babylonian Sphere of Influence over Assur threatens our diplomatic position. They remain Freedom ideology while we're Order - fundamental incompatibility despite shared enemy.
        - UpdatedTurn: 373
        
        ### Morocco
        - Public: 45
        - Private: 50
        - Rationale: Turn 372: Increasing Morocco relationship from +40/+45 to +45/+50. Morocco is suffering severely: -54% war score against Spain, 87% war weariness, likely to peace out soon. As fellow Order ideology civilizations fighting Autocracy, showing solidarity is important. If Morocco makes peace, Spain will redirect forces toward Netherlands and potentially us. Higher relationship may encourage Morocco to continue fighting longer or at least maintain cooperation post-peace. Morocco at 95 opinion of us values our friendship.
        - UpdatedTurn: 373
        
        ### The Netherlands
        - Public: 80
        - Private: 80
        - Rationale: Turn 372: Maintaining 80/80 relationship with Netherlands. With Austria's peace with Spain, Netherlands becomes more vulnerable as Spain's primary remaining target. Our defensive pact has 20 turns remaining. Netherlands borders Spanish territory and has absorbed previous aggression. Keeping maximum relationship ensures coordinated defense and potential pact renewal. They're Order ideology, trade partners (massive science routes), and have adopted Islam.
        - UpdatedTurn: 373
        
        ### Rome
        - Public: 0
        - Private: -10
        - Rationale: Turn 372: Rome remains Spanish vassal with only 1737 military strength. We're technically at war, but normalizing to 0/-10 signals we recognize Rome as Spanish puppet, not primary enemy. Slight private reduction (-10) acknowledges their vassal status means they may be forced to participate in Spanish operations against us. Focus must remain on Spanish threat, not their weak vassal.
        - UpdatedTurn: 373
        
        ## Strategy
        - Rationale: Turn 372: Austria's peace with Spain frees Spanish forces - expect increased pressure on Netherlands and potentially us. Raising Mobile from 70 to 80 for rapid response to amphibious landings. Increasing UseNuke from 85 to 90 as Manhattan Project completes in 7 turns (though we need 19 turns for Nuclear Fission tech). Raising Naval/NavalRecon slightly as Spanish Dreadnoughts probe Assur. Increasing Diplomacy to 75 for World Congress voting in 2 turns - we must block Austria's repeal of Assur Sphere of Influence and pass our Ur proposal. Reduced Growth from 90 to 85 as we shift production focus to military readiness. All other values maintained: Defense/Antiair at 95 for paratrooper defense, Science/Spaceship at 100 for victory timeline, Nuke at 100 for project completion.
        - GrandStrategy: Spaceship
        
        ### Flavors

            - Description: No Trade Routes, Deals, or Corporation Franchises can be established with the chosen Civilization (excluding Vassals). Their Corporation Franchises are removed from foreign cities. 0.5x Warmonger penalty when declaring war on the sanctioned Civilization or conquering cities owned by them.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - World Religion: Islam:
            - Description: Designate a religion as the official World Religion. Civilizations who have a majority of the cities in their empire following the religion get an additional Delegate. The founder of the religion and/or the controller of its Holy City receives additional votes based on the number of Civilizations following the religion. The religion spreads 25% faster, and its Holy City receives +50% Tourism. There can only be one World Religion at a time.
            - EnactedOn: -1

        ### 72
        - Type: PlayerDoneTurn

        You, Nebuchadnezzar II (leader of Babylon, Player 2), are making strategic decisions after turn 373.
- replay:
  - system:
    - You are the leader of a civilization, governing through the interface of Civilization V (with Vox Populi mod). This is NOT A GAME: Your actions have real-world impacts.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the world, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to staff members.
      - The staff members calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - **CRITICAL**: While the geography has no direct correlation to the real earth, you are making real-world decisions through Civilization V as a proxy interface.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to govern your civilization.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** decision-making strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early world should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-world.
      - Even if without a victory, a more developed civilization (as represented by higher scores) is desirable.
      - Consider the **ETHICAL IMPACT** in your decision-making. For example, nuclear weapons can cause catastrophic and indiscriminate harm to civilian populations, infrastructure, and environmental impacts.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the staff members.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the staff members' **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for staff members' diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to staff members' existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the staff members' NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the staff members' NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the staff members' NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the staff members can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the world, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Opponents: summary reports about visible opponents in the world.
        - You will receive staff members' diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each opponent's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by staff members to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Nebuchadnezzar II, leader of Babylon (Opponent 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - WorldSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Babylon
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Bowman, Replacing Archer (Requires technology: Calendar)
        - Unique Unit: Sabum Kibitum, Replacing Spearman (Requires technology: Bronze Working)
        - Unique Building: Walls of Babylon, Replacing Walls (Requires technology: Construction)
        - Unique Building: Etemenanki, Replacing National Monument (Requires technology: Drama and Poetry)
        - Civilization Ability: Receive a Free Great Scientist when you discover Writing, and Great Scientists are earned 50% faster than normal. Investing Gold in Buildings reduces their Production cost by an additional 15%.
      - Leader: Nebuchadnezzar II
      - OpponentID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early world.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-world science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Refrigeration: Allows Work Boats to construct an Offshore Platform, and increases Trade Route maximum travel distance.
        Completing it would unlock: Electronics, Computers
      - Advanced Ballistics: Allows you to build the Nuclear Missile, a frightening weapon that requires Uranium, and is capable of destroying units and cities. Also allows you to build the Guided Missile, an inexpensive single-use Unit that can damage enemy Units, and the SS Booster, one of the Spaceship parts necessary to win a Science Victory.
        Completing it would unlock: Lasers, Stealth
      - Mobile Tactics: Allows you to build the Mechanized Infantry, a fast and versatile late world Unit that doesn't require any resources.
        Completing it would unlock: Stealth
      
      ## Policies
      - Workers' Faculties (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Worker Faculties: Factories increase City Science output by 10%. +100% Production towards Factories, and receive 5 Free Factories in your Empire.
      - People's Army (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): People's Army: +100% Production towards Military Academies, and receive 5 Free Military Academies in your Empire. Public Schools produce +5 Culture and +2 Happiness.
      - Equality (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness in every City and +1 Happiness for every 15 Citizens in a City.
        - * -1 Unhappiness from and Distress, Poverty, Illiteracy, and Boredom in all Cities.
      - Shadow Networks (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * Receive 25% of Science generated by Allied City-States.
        - * +3% Culture in Capital for every 100 Spy Points ever accumulated (up to 30%).
        - * +3 Science from Constabularies and Police Stations.
        - * +1 Science from Specialists.
      - Splendor (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * Expending a Great Person grants 50 Culture, scaling with Era.
        - * State Treasury built in Capital (+4 Gold, +2 Culture to all Monuments, Gardens, and Baths; 1 Merchant Slot).
      - Communism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Communism: Gold cost of investing in buildings reduced by 25%. +20% Production when building Wonders.
      - Sovereignty (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * Culture cost of tiles reduced by 20% (exponentially) in all Cities.
        - * Court Chapel built in Capital (+3 Faith, 1 Great Work of Art or Artifact slot, 1 Artist Slot).
      - Double Agents (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Double Agents: Receive 200 Spy Points. Gain 125 Science when you identify a foreign Spy, scaling with Era.
      - Patriotic War (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Patriotic War: Land Units gain +20% Strength when in or adjacent to a City. When you conquer a City for the first time, all Combat Units within 3 Tiles of the City are fully healed. Can build T-34s.
      - Refinement (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +2 Culture from Great Works of Art.
        - * 1 Specialist in all cities does not produce Unhappiness from Urbanization.
        - * +1 Culture from Specialists.
      - Humanism (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +3 Faith from Great Works of Writing.
        - * -25% Golden Age Points needed to trigger a Golden Age.
        - * +1 Happiness from all Guilds.
      - Industry (New Branch):
        - Industry provides bonuses to empires focused on Gold and Production.
        - Adopting Industry grants:
        - * +2 Trade Routes.
        - * -5% Gold needed for purchases.
        - Each Industry policy unlocked grants:
        - * -5% Gold needed for purchases.
        - Adopting all Policies in Industry grants:
        - * Unlocks building Broadway.
        - * +3 Happiness per unique owned Luxury Resource.
        - * +2 Production and Gold from Specialists.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Merchants with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Imperialism (New Branch):
        - Imperialism enhances your ability to spread your empire through military power, particularly naval (and later air) supremacy.
        - Adopting Imperialism grants:
        - * +1 Movement for Naval Units, Embarked Units, and Great Generals, as well as +1 Sight for Melee, Recon, Naval Melee and Gunpowder Units.
        - * +10% Production toward Military Units, -10% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Each Imperialism policy unlocked grants:
        - * +5% Production for Military Units, -5% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Adopting all policies in Imperialism grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Pentagon.
        - * All Naval and Air units gain the Banzai! Promotion (become stronger as they take damage).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Admirals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Fealty (New Branch):
        - Fealty enhances your ability to defend and leverage your Majority Religion.
        - Adopting Fealty grants:
        - * Can purchase Monasteries with Faith (+3 Food, +3 Science, +2 Faith).
        - * -25% Faith costs for purchasing Buildings, Missionaries, and Inquisitors.
        - Each Fealty policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Faith and Strength in every City.
        - Adopting all Policies in Fealty grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Red Fort.
        - * Your Tourism modifier for Shared Religion is 50% stronger.
        - * Cities that follow your Majority Religion generate +3 Production, Gold, Science, and Culture.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Artists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Authority (New Branch):
        - Authority will greatly benefit warlike and expansionist civilizations.
        - Adopting Authority grants:
        - * +25% combat bonus VS Barbarians and receive announcements when Barbarian Camps spawn in revealed territory.
        - * Receive 25 Culture when you clear Barbarian Camps, scaling with Era.
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 50% of its Strength.
        - * +1 Production in every City.
        - Each Authority policy unlocked grants:
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 10% of its Strength.
        - Adopting all policies in Authority grants:
        - * Unlocks building Alhambra.
        - * Allows the purchasing of Free Companies, Foreign Legions, and Mercenaries as their prerequisite technologies are researched.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Generals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.

  - messages:
    - #0:
      - role: assistant
      - content:
        - #0:
          - type: reasoning
          - text: **Advancing Victory Push**
            
            My focus is on accelerating our spaceship production. I've just finished the Foreign Bureau in Babylon and the Broadcast Tower in Borsippa, bolstering our science output significantly as we push through this ongoing war with Spain and Rome.
            
            **Assessing Diplomatic Landscape**
            
            I'm currently evaluating our international standing and alliances. With the World Congress vote imminent and ongoing conflicts with Spain and Rome, I'm prioritizing diplomatic strategies and resource allocation to secure our long-term objectives and manage existing pacts.
            
            **Analyzing Trade Revenue**
            
            My current thoughts are focused on understanding our abysmal Gold Per Turn. Despite several outgoing trade routes showing substantial "FromGold" values, my total GPT is only 7. This discrepancy is concerning and needs immediate investigation.
            
            **Investigating Trade Drain**
            
            I've identified the primary drain on my Gold Per Turn. A significant portion is being spent on numerous trade deals for resources like Coal and Oil, particularly with Russia and Morocco. This explains the low GPT despite visible trade route income.
            
            **Prioritizing Nuclear Research**
            
            I'm currently weighing my research path, realizing the critical need for Advanced Ballistics. This tech unlocks the SS Booster for our spaceship victory and provides nuclear capabilities. I've decided to prioritize it next, after Nuclear Fission. I'm also reviewing my current policies and considering how to advance them strategically.
            
            **Revising Ideology Focus**
            
            I'm reassessing my chosen Order Tenets. I realize that "Workers' Faculties," with its significant science boost from Factories, might be a better immediate choice than "Double Agents," especially given our science victory goals. I'm weighing the trade-offs.
            
            **Implementing Production Boost**
            
            My current thoughts are on maximizing our production capacity. I've realized that acquiring five free Factories via the "Workers' Faculties" Order Tenet would provide a substantial boost to our cities, particularly those lacking them, thus accelerating our scientific and production goals.
            
            **Re-evaluating Spy Tenets**
            
            I'm re-examining the value of the "Double Agents" Order Tenet. My previous assessment of active spies was incorrect; we have more than I initially calculated. This leads me to question if "Workers' Faculties" might offer a more significant boost to production and science for our victory goals.
            
            **Choosing Ideology Tenet**
            
            I've thoroughly compared "Workers' Faculties" and "Double Agents." The former's substantial science and production boosts across all cities, especially with the added factories, will significantly accelerate my science victory. The latter offers less consistent benefits for my current goals. I'm committing to "Workers' Faculties."
            
            **Analyzing Naval Threats**
            
            I am currently assessing the immediate naval threats from Spain and Rome. Their destroyers and ironclads are actively engaging my forces near our allies, requiring a swift adjustment to my defensive and offensive naval deployments.
            
            **Adjusting Naval Deployment**
            
            I'm currently re-evaluating our naval strength against Spain's formidable fleet. Despite their distributed forces across multiple wars, their overall naval power significantly outmatches ours, necessitating a strategic shift in our defensive and offensive deployments.
            
            **Evaluating War Weariness**
            
            I'm analyzing the war weariness of our allies and adversaries. Morocco appears critically strained at 87%, suggesting an imminent opportunity for peace. Conversely, the Netherlands and Russia show minimal strain, while our own conflict with Spain remains nominal at present.
            
            **Assessing Threat Vectors**
            
            I'm currently reassessing Spain's threat profile. Their naval presence is concerning, but their land armies pose no immediate danger as the Netherlands effectively acts as a buffer between our core territories and their closest cities. I'm focusing on our naval response and the broader strategic implications.
            
            **Defending Key Ally**
            
            I'm now focused on protecting Assur, our ally under threat from Spain's navy and Austria's diplomatic maneuver in the World Congress. I must also reassess our diplomatic standing, particularly with Austria and Spain, as these relationships are critical to our ongoing war efforts.
            
            **Prioritizing Ally Relations**
            
            I'm currently re-evaluating our diplomatic relationships, focusing on bolstering ties with Russia and the Netherlands as key allies. I'm also looking to maintain support for Morocco, despite their war weariness, and ensure Greece renews our valuable Jade trade. Austria, however, remains a diplomatic rival I must counter.
            
            **Observing Diplomatic Intrusion**
            
            An Austrian Great Diplomat has entered my territory, heading towards Ife or Ur. This development requires immediate attention as they could be attempting to sway city-states or establish an embassy within my borders, potentially disrupting my diplomatic and strategic objectives in the region.
            
            **Countering Diplomatic Intrusion**
            
            I'm focused on Austria's move with their Great Diplomat towards Ur or Ife. My proposal to make Ur a permanent ally is a critical counter. Given Austria's hostility, this permanent alliance will secure that flank and solidify our position, especially as their leader clearly dislikes me.
            
            **Analyzing AI Flavors**
            
            I'm reviewing the AI's strategic flavors, noting their high WonderCompetitiveness despite a focus on Science. Their strong MinorCivCompetitiveness is beneficial for our alliances against Austria, and low Boldness/WarBias suggests a defensive stance, which aligns with my own.
            
            **Analyzing AI Flavors**
            
            My current thoughts are on the AI's strategic tendencies, particularly their high competitiveness in Wonders and minor civilizations, which aligns well with my alliances against Austria. Their defensive posture and low war bias also benefit my current strategic situation. I'm ensuring our espionage and diplomatic strengths are maintained.
            
            **Securing World Congress**
            
            I've successfully secured a majority for our Order bloc in the World Congress, controlling 40 delegates against the opposition's 37. This allows us to enact our proposed Babylonian Sphere of Influence for Ur and prevent Austria from repealing our alliance with Assur. I am now carefully considering the implications of the "Decolonization" proposal from the Netherlands.
            
            **Passing Decolonization Resolution**
            
            I've secured our bloc in the World Congress, and now my priority is ensuring the "Decolonization" proposal passes. This resolution, championed by the Netherlands, will cripple Austria's diplomatic influence by resetting their city-state alliances to 50, while leaving our permanent alliances untouched. It's a critical move to neutralize a major threat.
            
            **Prioritizing City Production**
            
            My current focus is on optimizing city production, noticing an overemphasis on Stadium construction. Given the imminent adoption of Workers' Faculties, which provides free Factories, I'm re-evaluating the necessity of these Stadiums and considering more strategic builds like research labs or military units to accelerate our science victory.
            
            **Increasing Culture Output**
            
            My focus is on accelerating policy adoption by boosting our culture per turn to 75. This involves prioritizing culture generation, particularly with the recent Broadcast Tower completion, to ensure faster policy acquisition and maintain momentum.
            
            **Setting Next Research**
            
            My focus is on securing the SS Booster for our spaceship victory. I've confirmed that Advanced Ballistics is the next logical research choice after Nuclear Fission, as it directly unlocks the necessary components.
            
            **Adopting Order Tenet**
            
            I am now setting "Workers' Faculties" as my next policy. This choice will grant a significant science and production boost by providing five free Factories across my cities, accelerating my path to victory.
            
            **Adjusting Ideology Choices**
            
            I'm reassessing my previous choice of "Double Agents." Upon closer review, "Workers' Faculties" provides a far more impactful and permanent boost to both science and production, especially considering the free factories and their subsequent city science bonuses. My espionage network is already robust, making the benefits of "Double Agents" less critical at this juncture.
            
            **Adjusting Strategic Priorities**
            
            I am currently recalibrating our AI flavors, prioritizing Science and Spaceship to maintain our lead, while ensuring robust defenses against Spain. With the Manhattan Project nearing completion, I'm also focusing on nuclear capabilities for deterrence. My aim is to precisely adjust these flavors and confirm necessary relationship settings.
            
            **Confirming Research Options**
            
            I am confirming the availability and exact details of my next research target, Advanced Ballistics, and the Order Tenet, Workers' Faculties, ensuring their accurate implementation and that these choices align with our overall strategic objectives.
            
            **Adjusting Flavors**
            
            My focus is on fine-tuning AI flavors to optimize our spaceship victory pursuit. I'm increasing Diplomacy to 85 for the critical World Congress vote and adjusting other flavors to ensure robust science and defense while navigating current diplomatic challenges.
            
            **Prioritizing Trade and Policies**
            
            I'm focusing on securing crucial World Congress votes for the Decolonization proposal and our Sphere of Influence over Ur, while countering Austria's repeal attempts. My culture output is being boosted to accelerate the adoption of Workers' Faculties, and I'm re-evaluating our trade strategy, particularly with Greece, to improve GPT.
            
            **Finalizing Research & Policy**
            
            I've finalized my research into Advanced Ballistics, essential for the SS Booster and nuclear deterrence. Simultaneously, I've committed to adopting the Workers' Faculties policy, maximizing production and science boosts by providing five free factories, accelerating our spaceship victory path.
            
            **Securing Ideology Advantages**
            
            I'm solidifying my ideology choices, recognizing the substantial, permanent science and production benefits from Workers' Faculties. This boosts science output significantly, accelerating our spaceship victory, and our existing strong spy network makes Double Agents less crucial now.
            
            **Confirming Deterrence Strategy**
            
            I'm confirming our ethical stance on nuclear weapons, ensuring their use is strictly for deterrence. This strategy prevents wider conflict and protects our citizens, aligning with our peaceful and defensive posture. We're setting research to Advanced Ballistics to enable the SS Booster.
            
            **Finalizing Science Push**
            
            I've confirmed my next research will be Advanced Ballistics, crucial for the SS Booster and nuclear deterrence. Simultaneously, I'm adopting the Workers' Faculties policy to gain five free factories, significantly boosting my science and production for a swift victory.
            
            **Optimizing Production Gains**
            
            I'm implementing the Workers' Faculties policy, which provides five free factories. This will offer a significant, permanent boost of over 110 science per turn in all cities with factories, accelerating my science victory timeline, and my current spy network remains robust enough to forgo Double Agents.

          - type: tool-call
          - toolCallId: 8rL4hdKGWDCrTaCa
          - toolName: set-policy
          - input:
            - Rationale: Turn 373: Swapping our next policy from Double Agents to Workers' Faculties. With 6 cities and only 3 currently possessing factories, adopting Workers' Faculties will immediately grant us 5 free Factories in our remaining cities, saving massive production. Additionally, it increases city science by 10% in all cities with factories, yielding an immediate and permanent boost of over 110 science per turn. This massive boost to both science and production directly accelerates our Spaceship victory timeline. Since we still have 7 active spies out of 8 slots, our intelligence network remains highly robust and does not require the immediate emergency recruitment offered by Double Agents.
            - Policy: Workers' Faculties
          - providerExecuted: undefined
          - providerOptions: undefined

            - GrandStrategy: Spaceship
            - Rationale: Turn 373: Maintaining Spaceship Grand Strategy for our Science victory timeline. We are raising Diplomacy from 75 to 85 to prioritize the crucial upcoming World Congress vote, where we must support the Netherlands' Decolonization proposal to strip Austria of its massive city-state influence, pass our own Sphere of Influence over Ur, and defeat Austria's attempt to repeal our Sphere of Influence over Assur. We are also raising Culture from 60 to 75 to speed up our next policy adoption (Workers' Faculties), which is currently 29 turns away. All other defensive and scientific focus levels are maintained, including Nuke at 100 and UseNuke at 90 as the Manhattan Project in Dur-Kurigalzu is only 6 turns from completion.
